<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Hlk192174583"></a><a name="Topic_598298"></a><a name="_Toc508685870"></a><a name="_Ref533415527"></a><a name="_Ref45159100"></a><a name="_Ref45159104"></a><a name="_Ref132442112"></a><a name="_Ref132442114"></a><a name="_Toc132442210"></a><a name="_Toc187123514"></a><a name="_Toc360912531"></a><a name="_Topic_433403_20240724115757_title"></a><a name="_Toc173868862"></a><a name="_Topic_556985_20250305111360_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598298_20250610144536_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139883"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>1 </span>概述</a></h1>
<p class=MsoNormal><a name="_Hlk173397151">H3C U-Center 5.0</a>提供企业级统一运维解决方案，涵盖“监、管、控、服、析”各业务领域，实现“云、网、端、安”ICT全域资源统一管理；支持对海量资源“直采直控”，同时支持对新华三运营、管理软件和第三方运维工具进行整合，为运维人员提供统一门户、统一告警、统一资源、统一流程、统一分析的全面运维解决方案。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>本文的目的是提供精细化安装部署指导方法，帮助市场及一线人员或用户准确地部署U-Center 5.0运行环境。</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>
</span>
<div class=WordSection3 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598299"></a><a name="_Topic_598299_20250610144911_title"></a><a name="_Toc190163609"></a><a name="_Topic_473569_20240808140099_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556986_20250305111742_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139884"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>2 </span>安装流程</a></h1>
<p class=MsoNormal>U-Center 5.0支持1个节点的单机部署和3个Master节点+N个Worker节点（N≥0）的集群部署两种部署模式。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>部署流程如<a href="#_Ref64562714">表2-1</a>所示。</p>

<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598300"></a><a name="_Toc68947137"></a><a name="_Topic_433405_20240724115905_title"></a><a name="_Toc173868864"></a><a name="_Topic_556987_20250305112128_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598300_20250610145257_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139885"></a><a name="_Toc398294018"></a><a name="_Toc398282501"></a><a name="_Toc398282327"></a><a name="_Toc398282153"></a><a name="_Toc398294017"></a><a name="_Toc398282500"></a><a name="_Toc398282326"></a><a name="_Toc398282152"></a><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>3 </span>安装前的准备工作</h1>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598301"></a><a name="_Ref56505596"></a><a name="_Toc68947140"></a><a name="_Topic_433408_20240724110313_title"></a><a name="_Toc185338899"></a><a name="_Topic_473573_20240808140395_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556988_20250305112585_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598301_20250610145571_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139886"></a><a name="_Toc408385256"></a>3.1  软硬件配置需求</h2>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598302"></a><a name="_Ref173401011"></a><a name="_Ref173401008"></a><a name="_Toc68947141"></a><a name="_Toc183185357"></a><a name="_Topic_473574_20240808140466_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556989_20250305113017_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598302_20250610145948_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139887">3.1.1  硬件配置需求</a></h3>
<p class=MsoNormal>硬件配置需求可通过以下方式获取：</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</a></span>中输入数据后获取评估结果，该评估结果用于参考。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;联系一线工程师帮忙评估。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_0"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 2" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307309_x_Img_x_png_0_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>为了确保业务的正常运行，必须将服务器的CPU供电模式设置为高性能（performance）模式，并关闭RAID卡的PR（Patrol Read，巡读）和CC（Consistency Check，一致性校验）功能（如不支持PRCC，则无需关闭）。具体操作方式请参见服务器/RAID卡相关产品手册，或联系服务器/RAID卡的生产厂家技术支持人员。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598303"></a><a name="_Toc195283868"></a><a name="_Topic_473575_20240808140534_title"></a><a name="_Toc68947142"></a><a name="_Toc185338901"></a><a name="_Topic_556990_20250305113492_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598303_20250610140341_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139888">3.1.2  操作系统要求</a></h3>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_1"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 3" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307310_x_Img_x_png_1_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>集群中所有节点必须安装相同版本的操作系统。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Hlk195283841"></a><a name="_Hlk201655491"></a><a name="Topic_609215"></a><a name="_Topic_556992_20250305115129_title"></a><a name="_Toc185338898"></a><a name="_Ref136508603"></a><a name="_Ref136508607"></a><a name="_Topic_473572_20240808140340_title"></a><a name="_Toc195283870"></a><a name="_Topic_609215_20250619113493_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139890">3.2  获取软件包</a></h2>
<p class=MsoNormal>此处以H3C自研的NingOS操作系统为例进行说明。当选择其它操作系统时，NingOS的ISO文件不是必选项。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>安装包的名称格式如<a href="#_Ref54861888">表3-2</a>所示，其中<span class=ItalicText>version</span>为版本号，<span class=ItalicText>platform</span>为CPU架构类型。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>说明列的“必选”意为：如需正常运行U-Center 5.0，必须安装的应用包；“可选”意为：可根据需求安装应用包，如不安装，将无该功能。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_2"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 1" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307321_x_Img_x_png_2_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>不同的组件、不同的版本可能仅发布x86或ARM安装包，具体请以版本发布文件为准。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>

<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598306"></a><a name="_Toc185338905"></a><a name="_Topic_473579_20240808140784_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556993_20250305115556_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598306_20250610141958_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139891">3.3  验证软件包</a></h2>
<p class=MsoNormal>在安装软件前，请参考如下命令对软件包执行MD5验证，确保软件包的完整性和正确性。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;查看已上传的安装包。</p>
<p class=TerminalDisplayIndent1>[root@node1~]# cd /opt/matrix/app/install/packages/</p>
<p class=TerminalDisplayIndent1>[root@node1~]# ls</p>
<p class=TerminalDisplayIndent1>ITOM-IOM_E7301_x86.zip     </p>
<p class=ItemIndent1>…略…</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;查看安装包的MD5值，以ITOM-IOM_E7301_x86.zip为例。</p>
<p class=TerminalDisplayIndent1>[root@node1~]# md5sum ITOM-IOM_E7301_x86.zip</p>
<p class=TerminalDisplayIndent1>bf6e49e3e8ef1fc562a3a84f12349859 
ITOM-IOM_E7301_x86.zip</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;将上述步骤中获取到的MD5值与发布的软件版本文件MD5值进行比对，若一致，则软件包正确。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598307"></a><a name="_Topic_539884_20250123160389_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556994_20250305115923_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598307_20250610142259_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139892">3.4  安装规划</a></h2>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Toc68947187"></a><a name="Topic_598308"></a><a name="_Toc193808173"></a><a name="_Topic_473576_20240808140585_title"></a><a name="_Toc185338902"></a><a name="_Topic_567105_20250325155488_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598308_20250610142581_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139893">3.4.1  磁盘分区</a></h3>
<p class=MsoNormal>请按照<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</span></a>计算结果中的“单台Master节点本地存储空间”数据配置磁盘分区。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>seasqlplus-uc分区说明：</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;数据盘seasqlplus-uc主要用于保存历史监控性能数据。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;硬件资源计算工具自动计算是否需要配置seasqlplus-uc：</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;当等效网元数量小于2000时（等效网元数量=网管管理网元数+IOM管理资源数+NTA接口数量*10），不需要配置seasqlplus-uc独立分区。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;当等效网元数量大于2000时，需要配置seasqlplus-uc分区并挂载独立磁盘。原因是：当业务量较大时，seasqlplus-uc可能会导致较高的磁盘IO，如果与其他业务共用一块磁盘，可能会影响其他业务的正常运行。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;seasqlplus-uc挂载独立磁盘后的剩余空间不能分配给其他分区使用。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;seasqlplus-uc挂载独立磁盘的方法见<a href="#_Ref173403792">11 1. </a><a href="#_Ref173403792">如何为seasqlplus-uc挂载独立磁盘？</a></p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598309"></a><a name="_Topic_598309_20250610142952_title"></a><a name="_Toc190163619"></a><a name="_Topic_539886_20250123160216_title"></a><a name="_Topic_539888_20250123164611_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556996_20250305110754_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139894">3.4.2  网络规划</a></h3>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'>1. 组网规划</h4>
<p class=MsoNormal>U-Center 5.0在Matrix集群部署模式下，至少需要3个Master节点+N个Worker节点（N≥0，N根据任务负载情况决定），典型组网规划如下所示：</p>
<p class=FigureDescription style='text-indent:0cm'>图3-1 组网规划</p>
<p class=Figure><a name="x_Img_x_png_3"><img border=0 width=567 height=333 id="图片 39" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307332_x_Img_x_png_3_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Topic_473571_20240808140220_title">2. IP</a>地址规划</h4>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_4"><img border=0 width=65 height=27 id="Drawing 0" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307343_x_Img_x_png_4_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>若具体业务场景需要多张网卡，有如下注意事项：1、部署集群前的检查，集群使用的网卡需要是ifconfig命令检测到的第一块网卡。2、如果不满足上述条件，则需要保证集群使用的是之前配置的网卡，并满足以下条件之一：</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;条件1：网卡down状态且onboot=no。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;条件2：网卡UP状态，已配置IP，且需要配置安全策略。</p>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>当集群的南北向网络使用不同网卡但处于同一VLAN网段时，应在南向网卡配置中设置NOZEROCONF=yes。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>U-Center 5.0支持IPv4部署，IP地址规划如<a href="#_Ref54861880">表3-3</a>所示。</p>
<p class=TableDescription style='text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Ref54861880">表3-3 组网明细</a></p>
<table class=Table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=0 width=601  style='layout-grid:none;margin-left:36.85pt;border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>

  <tr>

   </td>

<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598310"></a><a name="_Toc173868872"></a><a name="_Topic_473578_20240808140729_title"></a><a name="_Topic_556999_20250305113326_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598310_20250610145189_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139895">3.5  安装环境确认</a></h2>
<p class=MsoNormal><a name="_Toc54787755"></a><a name="_Toc54787799"></a><a name="_Toc54860565"></a><a name="_Toc54861851"></a><a name="_Toc54862826"></a><a name="_Toc55377260"></a><a name="_Toc55380345"></a><a name="_Toc55380812"></a><a name="_Toc55393658"></a><a name="_Toc54787756"></a><a name="_Toc54787800"></a><a name="_Toc54860566"></a><a name="_Toc54861852"></a><a name="_Toc54862827"></a><a name="_Toc55377261"></a><a name="_Toc55380346"></a><a name="_Toc55380813"></a><a name="_Toc55393659"></a><a name="_Toc54787757"></a><a name="_Toc54787801"></a><a name="_Toc54860567"></a><a name="_Toc54861853"></a><a name="_Toc54862828"></a><a name="_Toc55377262"></a><a name="_Toc55380347"></a><a name="_Toc55380814"></a><a name="_Toc55393660"></a>安装环境请参考<a href="#_Ref529516913">表3-4</a>中所示的各个检测项目，确保安装U-Center 5.0的条件已经具备。</p>
<p class=TableDescription style='text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Ref529516913"></a><a name="_Toc131396184"></a><a name="_Toc85979384"></a><a name="_Ref41723970"></a><a name="_Toc34041796"></a><a name="_Toc29028550">表3-4 安装环境确认</a></p>
<table class=Table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=0 width=601  style='layout-grid:none;margin-left:36.85pt;border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
 <thead>
  <tr style='height:18.3pt'>
   <td width=192 colspan=2 style='width:144.35pt;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;    border-left:none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;    background:#D9D9D9;padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;height:18.3pt'>
   <p class=TableHeading>检测项</p>
   </td>
   <td width=408 style='width:306.35pt;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;    border-left:none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:none;    background:#D9D9D9;padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt;height:18.3pt'>

   </td>
 </thead>
 <tr>
  <td width=99 rowspan=2 style='width:74.2pt;border-top:none;border-left:none;   border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>服务器或虚拟机</p>
  </td>
  <td width=94 style='width:70.15pt;border-top:none;border-left:none;   border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>硬件检查</p>
  </td>
  <td width=408 style='width:306.35pt;border:none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;   padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>请检查硬件是否符合要求（包括CPU、内存、硬盘、网卡等）</p>
  </td>
 <tr>
  <td width=94 style='width:70.15pt;border-top:none;border-left:none;   border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>软件检查</p>
  </td>
  <td width=408 style='width:306.35pt;border:none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;   padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>请检查如下几点：</p>
  <p class=ItemListinTable><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:   Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;操作系统版本符合要求</p>
  <p class=ItemListinTable><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:   Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;请检查系统时间已配置完成，建议配置NTP时间同步，整网使用统一的时钟源同步时间，且保证NTP服务器时间与当前时间一致</p>
  <p class=ItemListinTable><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:   Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;请确保服务器已配置磁盘阵列</p>
  </td>
 <tr>
  <td width=192 colspan=2 style='width:144.35pt;border-top:none;border-left:   none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:   0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>客户端</p>
  </td>
  <td width=408 style='width:306.35pt;border:none;border-bottom:solid gray 1.0pt;   padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>请确保浏览器版本符合要求</p>
  </td>
 <tr>
  <td width=192 colspan=2 style='width:144.35pt;border-top:none;border-left:   none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid gray 1.0pt;   padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>服务器和操作系统兼容性</p>
  </td>
  <td width=408 style='width:306.35pt;border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;   padding:0cm 5.4pt 0cm 5.4pt'>
  <p class=TableText>请参见以下链接，查看H3C服务器和操作系统的兼容性列表： <a href="http://www.h3c.com/cn/home/qr/default.htm?id=367"><span   class=Reference-R0G144B200>http://www.h3c.com/cn/home/qr/default.htm?id=367</a></span></p>
  </td>
</table>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_5"></a><a name="_Toc398282158"></a><a name="_Toc398282332"></a><a name="_Toc398282506"></a><a name="_Toc398294023"></a><img border=0 width=65 height=27 id="Drawing 5" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307347_x_Img_x_png_5_2589262_30005_0.png"></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;推荐将服务器或虚拟机的下一次启动模式配置为UEFI模式。其中VMware的引导固件，请选择下拉框中的EFI，不要勾选“是否为此虚拟机启用UEFI安全引导”选项。若安装的VMware是英文版本，请不要勾选“Secure Boot”选项。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装操作系统时，请勿同时使用KVM给多个服务器安装同一镜像。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;H5 KVM性能不稳定，可能出现加载镜像停止或缓慢等问题，建议使用Java KVM挂载镜像安装操作系统。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装过程中禁止输入Scroll Lock键，否则可能会导致安装失败。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装过程中异常断电会导致部分服务安装失败，请重新安装以保证功能完整性。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;U-Center 5.0安装部署过程中，请勿进行开启或关闭防火墙的操作。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;集群部署完成后，请勿修改系统时间，否则可能导致集群异常。<a <div class=WordSection5 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598311"></a><a name="_Topic_598311_20250610145505_title"></a><a name="_Toc174369378"></a><a name="_Topic_473580_20240808140862_title"></a><a name="_Toc195283876"></a><a name="_Topic_557000_20250305113671_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139896"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>4 </span>安装统一数字底盘</a></h1>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_6"><img border=0 width=66 height=27 id="图片 2" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307348_x_Img_x_png_6_2589262_30005_0.png" alt=注意></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>在非root环境（如admin环境）下，请在部署统一数字底盘应用前，使用root用户手动创建日志目录，并修改所有者，命令如下：</p>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>mkdir -p /var/log/matrix-diag/deploy/
&amp;&amp; chown admin:wheel /var/log/matrix-diag/deploy/</p>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>ll -d
/var/log/matrix-diag/deploy/</p>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>若集群存在多个节点，则所有节点均需执行同样的操作。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>部署U-Center 5.0前需要先部署统一数字底盘，在底盘的Matrix中部署U-Center 5.0组件。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>部署统一数字底盘的具体步骤请参见《H3C统一数字底盘部署指导》。</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>
</span>
<div class=WordSection6 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598312"></a><a name="_Toc173868875"></a><a name="_Topic_473581_20240808140909_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557001_20250305114059_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598312_20250610145803_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139897"></a><a name="_Ref142296687"></a><a name="_Ref142296684"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>5 </span>安装</a>U-Center 5.0<a name="_部署portal应用"></a></h1>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598313"></a><a name="_Toc183185365"></a><a name="_Ref183100406"></a><a name="_Ref183100409"></a><a name="_Topic_476033_20240812130178_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557002_20250305114410_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598313_20250610140104_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139898">5.1  安装说明</a></h2>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装时间会根据网络规模和组件的不同而有所变化，以单机小规模为例，通常需要0.6~0.7小时。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;底盘单机部署时，U-Center可以在标准模式或Proxy模式下进行部署，安装详细步骤分别见<a href="#_Ref183099982">5.2  </a><a href="#_Ref183099982">安装应用（标准模式）</a>和<a href="#_Ref183105097">5.3  </a><a href="#_Ref183105097">安装应用（Proxy模式）</a>。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;NSM详细安装步骤请见《H3C NSM 安装部署指导》。</p>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598314"></a><a name="_Toc195283879"></a><a name="_Topic_511075_20241121171403_title"></a><a name="_Ref183099982"></a><a name="_Toc183185366"></a><a name="_Topic_557003_20250305114761_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598314_20250610140415_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139899">5.2  安装应用（标准模式）</a></h2>
<p class=MsoNormal><a href="#_Ref54861888">表3-2</a>为U-Center组件相关的安装包，用户可以按需部署安装包。</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598315"></a><a name="_Topic_598315_20250610140771_title"></a><a name="_Toc195283880"></a><a name="_Topic_511076_20241121171131_title"></a><a name="_Toc183185367"></a><a name="_Topic_557004_20250305115186_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139900">5.2.1  上传安装包</a></h3>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_7"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307349_x_Img_x_png_7_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;融合部署页面可对指定应用进行查看详情、扩容、升级等操作。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;请通过Matrix前台页面进行安装包上传操作，严禁通过后台传包。如果在特殊网络环境下需要通过后台传包，请联系技术支持工程师。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在浏览器中访问标准模式的底盘环境“https://<i>ip_address</i>:8443/matrix/ui”，输入Matrix的用户名和密码（默认用户名为admin）登录Matrix。其中“<span class=ItalicText>ip_address</span>”为北向业务虚IP地址。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击[部署&gt;融合部署]菜单项，进入融合部署页面。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-1 融合部署</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_8"><img border=0 width=531 height=92 id="图片 19" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307350_x_Img_x_png_8_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;安装包管理&gt;按钮，进入安装包管理页面。该页面支持对融合部署安装包进行上传、删除等操作。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-2 安装包管理</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_9"><img border=0 width=532 height=258 id="图片 11" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307351_x_Img_x_png_9_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;上传&gt;按钮，在弹出窗口中单击&lt;选择文件&gt;按钮，选择一个或多个安装包文件后，单击&lt;上传&gt;按钮开始上传，上传完成后将自动回到安装包管理页面。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading1 style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_10"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307311_x_Img_x_png_10_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText1 style='margin-left:0cm'>上传安装包支持两种方式：</p>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;上传ITOM包：上传ITOM组件总包，上传后会自动解压成组件安装包。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;上传组件包：直接上传各组件安装包。</p>
</div>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-3 上传安装包</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_11"><img border=0 width=531 height=338 id="图片 78" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307312_x_Img_x_png_11_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598316"></a><a name="_Topic_598316_20250610141203_title"></a><a name="_Toc195283881"></a><a name="_Topic_511080_20241121173224_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557005_20250305115676_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139901">5.2.2  开始安装</a></h3>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装包上传完成后，返回融合部署页面。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;安装&gt;按钮，进入应用选择页面。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;场景选择：系统预置了3个场景，分别是U-Center统一运维场景、U-Center基础网管场景和U-Center ICT监控场景。用户可以按需选择单个或多个场景，系统将自动勾选已上传安装包且为该场景推荐的应用，用户可以根据需要取消或增加其他应用。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;自定义选择：用户可以根据需要自定义勾选一个或多个应用来进行安装。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading1 style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_12"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 8" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307313_x_Img_x_png_12_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;当场景推荐的应用已全部安装时，场景名字展示为置灰状态无法勾选。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;对于有依赖关系的应用，将自动勾选其所依赖的应用。如果依赖的应用未被部署或不在当前已有安装包内，则会提示相关信息。</p>
</div>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-4 应用选择</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_13"><img border=0 width=532 height=414 id="图片 16" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307314_x_Img_x_png_13_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;下一步&gt;按钮，进入安装包选择页面。系统将自动选择默认安装包，用户也可以手动选择，选择后系统将对安装包进行校验。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-5 安装包选择</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_14"><img border=0 width=531 height=350 id="图片 40" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307315_x_Img_x_png_14_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;校验通过后，单击&lt;下一步&gt;按钮，进入资源配置页面，选择合适的资源档位。资源档位根据<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</a></span>中的“资源档位”结果进行选择。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-6 资源配置</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_15"><img border=0 width=531 height=255 id="图片 47" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307316_x_Img_x_png_15_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(5)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;下一步&gt;按钮，进入参数配置页面。根据页面提示，有些应用无需配置参数。若需配置参数，用户需选择部署的节点。单机部署模式下只有一个节点且必选。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-7 参数配置</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_16"><img border=0 width=531 height=254 id="图片 50" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307317_x_Img_x_png_16_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(6)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;部署&gt;按钮，在弹出对话框中对依赖的中间节点和需要安装的应用进行确认，若在上一步骤有选择部署节点，还需确认应用节点。单击&lt;确定&gt;按钮，开始部署。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-8 参数确认</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_17"><img border=0 width=531 height=300 id="图片 51" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307318_x_Img_x_png_17_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-9 部署中</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_18"><img border=0 width=531 height=232 id="图片 52" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307319_x_Img_x_png_18_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(7)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;部署成功后，将自动回到融合部署页面，查看已部署的应用。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-10 查看已部署应用</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_19"><img border=0 width=531 height=340 id="图片 54" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307320_x_Img_x_png_19_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598317"></a><a name="_Topic_511081_20241121181247_title"></a><a name="_Hlk183104547"></a><a name="_Toc185338506"></a><a name="_Ref183105097"></a><a name="_Topic_557006_20250305110017_title"></a><a name="_Toc195283882"></a><a name="_Topic_598317_20250610141505_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139902">5.3  安装应用（Proxy</a>模式）</h2>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598318"></a><a name="_Topic_598318_20250610141855_title"></a><a name="_Toc185338913"></a><a name="_Topic_522198_20241217143601_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557007_20250305110414_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139903">5.3.1  Proxy</a>部署说明</h3>
<p class=MsoNormal>Proxy部署至少需准备两套环境：U-Center 5.0 Server（即按标准模式部署的底盘环境）和U-Center 5.0 Proxy（即按代理模式部署的底盘环境），Server为用户提供了统一的操作入口，用户所有的操作都在Server端进行，Proxy端只负责数据采集。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>Proxy部署模式下，仅支持安装部分U-Center组件，如下表所示，用户可以按需部署安装包。</p>
<p class=TableDescription style='text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Ref185340153">表5-1 Proxy</a>部署应用安装包</p>

<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_20"><img border=0 width=62 height=25 src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307322_x_Img_x_png_20_2589262_30005_0.png" alt=说明></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>如需在Proxy部署模式下使用Agent采集应用，必须安装BMP_UCP_Agent。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598319"></a><a name="_Toc195283884"></a><a name="_Topic_511082_20241121182941_title"></a><a name="_Toc185338507"></a><a name="_Topic_557008_20250305110881_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598319_20250610142206_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139904">5.3.2  <span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;color:windowtext'>‍‍</span></a>上传安装包</h3>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_21"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 7" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307323_x_Img_x_png_21_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText style='margin-left:0cm'>请通过Matrix前台页面进行安装包上传操作，严禁通过后台传包。如果在特殊网络环境下需要通过后台传包，请联系技术支持工程师。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在浏览器中访问Proxy模式的底盘环境“https://<i>ip_address</i>:8443/matrix/ui”，输入Matrix的用户名和密码（默认用户名为admin）登录Matrix。其中“<span class=ItalicText>ip_address</span>”为北向业务虚IP地址。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击[部署&gt;应用]菜单项，进入应用列表页面。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-11 应用列表</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_22"><img border=0 width=531 height=236 id="图片 56" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307324_x_Img_x_png_22_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;部署应用&gt;按钮，进入选择安装包页面。该页面支持对安装包进行上传、删除等操作。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-12 安装包管理</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_23"><img border=0 width=531 height=243 id="图片 57" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307325_x_Img_x_png_23_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;上传&gt;按钮，在弹出窗口中单击选择一个或多个安装包文件后，单击&lt;上传&gt;按钮开始上传。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-13 上传安装包</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_24"><img border=0 width=456 height=244 id="图片 61" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307326_x_Img_x_png_24_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598321"></a><a name="_Toc195283885"></a><a name="_Topic_511083_20241121184202_title"></a><a name="_Toc184226331"></a><a name="_Topic_557009_20250305111298_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598321_20250610142546_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139905">5.3.3  开始安装</a></h3>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装包上传完成后，选择需要安装的应用安装包，单击&lt;下一步&gt;按钮，开始解析安装包。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-14 选择安装包</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_25"><img border=0 width=530 height=278 id="图片 82" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307327_x_Img_x_png_25_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装包解析完成后，进入选择应用页面，确认已选择的应用。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-15 选择应用</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_26"><img border=0 width=531 height=240 id="图片 83" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307328_x_Img_x_png_26_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;下一步&gt;按钮，进入配置参数页面。查看当前应用软件包的各配置项及对应的配置项参数，并可按实际的网络规模和业务规模修改资源档位。资源档位根据<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</a></span>中的“资源档位”结果进行选择。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-16 配置参数</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_27"><img border=0 width=532 height=243 id="图片 84" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307329_x_Img_x_png_27_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;部署&gt;按钮，开始部署。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-17 部署中</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_28"><img border=0 width=531 height=61 id="图片 85" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307330_x_Img_x_png_28_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(5)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;部署成功后，单击&lt;确定&gt;按钮，将自动回到应用列表页面，查看已部署的应用。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-18 查看已部署应用</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_29"><img border=0 width=531 height=240 id="图片 86" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307331_x_Img_x_png_29_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<h3 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598322"></a><a name="_Toc195283886"></a><a name="_Topic_519049_20241204140114_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557010_20250305111661_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598322_20250610142850_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139906">5.3.4  启动region</a></h3>
<p class=MsoNormal>Proxy部署应用完成后，需启动region应用，确保U-Center中的“分级管理”菜单显示。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;前往Proxy关联的Server端，在顶部导航“观测”菜单下，单击[监控&gt;应用监控]菜单项，进入应用监控页面。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;展开BMP_Connect组件下的应用，单击region应用操作列的<a name="x_Img_x_png_30"><img border=0 width=22 height=17 id="图片 18" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307333_x_Img_x_png_30_2589262_30005_0.png"></a>按钮，开启region。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图5-19 开启region</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_31"><img border=0 width=531 height=245 id="图片 28" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307334_x_Img_x_png_31_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>

name="_Toc54714836"></a><a name="_Toc54712069"></a><a name="_Toc54708091"></a><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>6 </span>访问U-Center 5.0</h1>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在浏览器中输入U-Center 5.0的登录地址“http://<span class=ItalicText>ip_address:30000</span>”，其中“<span class=ItalicText>ip_address</span>”为配置的北向业务虚IP，30000为缺省端口号，进入如<a href="#_Ref533600380">图6-1</a>所示登录页面。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading1 style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_32"><img border=0 width=62 height=25 id="图片 1" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307335_x_Img_x_png_32_2589262_30005_0.png" alt=说明></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesText1 style='margin-left:0cm'>Proxy所在环境IP不支持访问U-Center，仅能通过其关联的Server端的IP进行访问。</p>
</div>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1><a name="_Ref533600380">图6-1 登录</a>U-Center 5.0</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_33"><img border=0 width=565 height=232 id="Drawing 9" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307336_x_Img_x_png_33_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;输入操作员名称和密码（默认用户名为admin）后，单击&lt;登录&gt;按钮，进入U-Center 5.0主页面。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;若在Proxy部署应用中已启动region，则在[系统&gt;系统配置]菜单下将会显示“分级管理”菜单，并可在该页面对管理站进行管理操作，例如增加Proxy站点并在增加监控资源时使用，具体操作请见页面联机帮助。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图6-2 分级管理菜单</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_34"><img border=0 width=530 height=120 id="图片 32" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307337_x_Img_x_png_34_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div>

name="_Toc53579989"></a><a name="_Toc53583421"></a><a name="_Toc53583732"></a><a name="_Toc53591465"></a>U-Center 5.0部署完成后，需要获取License授权后才能正常使用。已购买产品的用户，使用软件授权函中包含的授权码进行后续注册流程。如果是项目试用，请联系H3C相关市场人员进行试用授权申请获取相关授权。</p>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598325"></a><a name="_Toc195283889"></a><a name="_Topic_473586_20240808141216_title"></a><a name="_Toc188548110"></a><a name="_Topic_557013_20250305112875_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598325_20250610143754_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139909">7.1  License</a>支持情况说明</h2>
<p class=MsoNormal>License支持情况说明介绍了U-Center 5.0具体各个组件功能需要通过License授权以及License授权的相关属性，请参见<a href="http://www.h3c.com/cn/home/qr/default.htm?id=927">《H3C AD-NET&amp;U-Center方案License支<span lang=EN-US>持情况说明》</a></span>中的“U-Center”章节。</p>
<h2 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598326"></a><a name="_Toc195283890"></a><a name="_Topic_473587_20240808141341_title"></a><a name="_Topic_433430_20240724112018_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557014_20250305113287_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598326_20250610144071_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139910">7.2  安装并获取授权</a></h2>
<p class=MsoNormal>关于授权的申请和安装详细过程，请参见<a href="http://www.h3c.com/cn/home/qr/default.htm?id=636">《H3C软件产品远程授权License使用指南》</a>。License文件导入H3C License Server授权管理系统后，需配置U-Center 5.0统一运维平台（作为License Client）使其与License Server联通。</p>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'>1. 创建License Server客户端</h4>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;登录H3C License Server授权管理系统，单击[首页&gt;客户端管理&gt;客户端配置]菜单项，进入客户端配置页面。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;增加&gt;按钮，进入增加客户端页面，配置客户端名称与客户端密码。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击&lt;确认&gt;按钮，增加客户端。</p>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'>2. 配置客户端连接</h4>
<p class=MsoNormal>用于配置客户端连接License Server并获取授权。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;登录U-Center 5.0统一运维平台。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;选择“系统”页签，在左导航中单击[License管理&gt;License信息]菜单项。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在License Server信息区域的输入框中输入License Server IP地址等参数，单击&lt;连接&gt;按钮，开始与License server建立连接。参数说明如下：</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;IP地址：License Server所在服务器的IP地址。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;端口号：缺省值为“5555”，与License Server授权服务端口号保持一致。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;客户端名称：License Server中设置的客户端名称。</p>
<p class=ItemList2 style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:70.9pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:6.0pt;font-family:Wingdings'>¡</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;客户端密码：License Server中设置的客户端名称对应的密码。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;成功建立连接即可从License server上获取授权。连接状态显示“连接成功”后，等待一会儿，单击<a name="x_Img_x_png_35"><img border=0 width=15 height=15 id="Drawing 11" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307338_x_Img_x_png_35_2589262_30005_0.png"></a>按钮可刷新当前License信息。</p>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading1 style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_36"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 12" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307339_x_Img_x_png_36_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:2.0cm;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;启动客户端服务时，需保证License Server的防火墙处于关闭状态，否则可能造成License Server不可连接。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList1 style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;启动客户端服务时，需保证License Server有未被申请的授权，否则可能造成License Client服务取不到任何授权。</p>
</div>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'>3. 查看License使用状况</h4>
<p class=MsoNormal>登录License Server授权管理系统，单击“客户端管理&gt;客户端连接”菜单项，进入客户端连接信息页面。该页面用于显示License Server的客户端连接信息，并可对客户端连接进行管理，如进行强制下线等操作。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在列表中单击最左侧的复选框选中指定客户端，单击&lt;下线客户端&gt;按钮，可批量下线已选中的所有客户端，或单击&lt;下线全部客户端&gt;按钮，可下线所有客户端。</p>
<p class=ItemList style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在“操作”区段中单击“详情”图标，进入客户端连接详情页面。单击授权列表操作列“强制回收授权”图标，可以强制回收该授权，或单击&lt;强制回收所有授权&gt;按钮，可以强制回收分配给该客户端的所有授权。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>
</span>

<div class=WordSection10 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598328"></a><a name="_Topic_598328_20250610144620_title"></a><a name="_Topic_433432_20240724112154_title"></a><a name="_Toc173868883"></a><a name="_Topic_557016_20250305113981_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139912"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>9 </span>集群故障重建与恢复</a></h1>
<p class=MsoNormal>若集群节点服务器的硬件出现故障，导致节点服务器运行异常且无法恢复时，需要更换新的节点服务器，或节点服务器操作系统异常，都需在原服务器上重新安装操作系统和Matrix。在统一数字底盘中可对集群执行故障恢复操作，具体步骤请参见《H3C统一数字底盘部署指导》。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>
</span>
<div class=WordSection11 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="Topic_598329"></a><a name="_Toc195283893"></a><a name="_Topic_473590_20240808141528_title"></a><a name="_Toc173868884"></a><a name="_Topic_557017_20250305114464_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598329_20250610144984_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139913"><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family:,serif'>10 </span>卸载U-Center 5.0</a></h1>
<div style='border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesHeading style='margin-left:0cm'><a name="x_Img_x_png_37"><img border=0 width=61 height=25 id="Drawing 13" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307340_x_Img_x_png_37_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
</div>
<div style='border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm; margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm'>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;当使用直接卸载Matrix的方式来卸载U-Center 5.0时，底盘组件本身的卸载处理并不会被触发执行，会存在残留数据，重新安装底盘之前，需要清空所有Matrix节点/var/lib/ssdata/目录下的内容。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;卸载模块或单个应用包会导致相关数据被删除，卸载前需确认该模块或应用是否正在被使用，请谨慎执行卸载操作。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;如需保留组件配置和数据，请在卸载前使用备份功能，请参见“<a href="#_Ref67319395">8 </a><a href="#_Ref67319395">备份恢复</a>”。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;如果要卸载的应用被其他应用所依赖，系统会一并勾选依赖于该应用的所有应用，以保证应用运行的完整性。此情况下，无法单独卸载该应用。</p>
<p class=NotesTextList style='margin-left:19.85pt'><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol'>·</span>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;此处以在标准模式下卸载U-Center 5.0为例进行介绍。</p>
</div>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‍在浏览器中访问“https://<i>ip_address</i>:8443/matrix/ui”，输入用户名和密码（默认用户名为admin）登录Matrix。其中“<span class=ItalicText>ip_address</span>”为北向业务虚IP地址。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;单击[部署&gt;融合部署]菜单项，进入融合部署页面。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在列表中勾选一个或多个应用后，单击&lt;卸载&gt;按钮。</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;在弹出提示框中单击&lt;确定&gt;按钮，即可卸载应用。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图10-1 卸载应用</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_38"><img border=0 width=532 height=254 id="图片 55" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307341_x_Img_x_png_38_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div>
<span lang=EN-US style='font-size:10.5pt;font-family:"Arial",sans-serif'><br clear=all style='page-break-before:always'>
</span>
<div class=WordSection12 style='layout-grid:15.85pt'>
<h1 style='margin-left:0cm;text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Hlk185341849"></a><a name="Topic_598330"></a><a name="_Toc185338932"></a><a name="_Topic_473599_20240808141927_title"></a><a name="_Topic_557026_20250305111798_title"></a><a name="_Topic_598330_20250610145306_title"></a><a name="_Toc203139914"></a><a name="_Toc172883823"></a><span lang=EN-US style='font-size:40.0pt;font-family: ,serif'>11 </span>常见问题解答</h1>
<h4 style='text-indent:0cm'><a name="_Ref173403792"></a><a name="_Ref53415366">1. 如何为</a>seasqlplus-uc挂载独立磁盘？</h4>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(1)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;为seasqlplus-uc每个节点分别准备一块独立的磁盘。磁盘容量基于<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</span></a>计算结果中的数据盘“/var/lib/ssdata/middleware/seasqlplus-uc”大小确定。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图11-1 准备独立磁盘</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_39"><img border=0 width=531 height=329 id="图片 34" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307342_x_Img_x_png_39_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(2)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;安装操作系统时，选择已准备的独立磁盘。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图11-2 选择磁盘</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_40"><img border=0 width=529 height=397 id="图片 33" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307344_x_Img_x_png_40_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(3)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;为分区/var/lib/ssdata/middleware/seasqlplus-uc挂载独立磁盘，期望容量为<a href="https://iservice.h3c.com/#/resourceCalculate"><span class=Reference-R0G144B200>硬件资源计算工具</span></a>计算结果中该分区计算的大小，文件系统类型为ext4。修改设备，选择准备的独立磁盘配置挂载点。</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图11-3 手动分区</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_41"><img border=0 width=529 height=397 id="图片 48" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307345_x_Img_x_png_41_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=ItemIndent1>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=FigureDescriptionIndent1>图11-4 配置挂载点</p>
<p class=FigureIndent1><a name="x_Img_x_png_42"><img border=0 width=529 height=397 id="图片 49" src="https://resource.h3c.com/cn/202507/14/20250714_14307346_x_Img_x_png_42_2589262_30005_0.png"></a></p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
<p class=ItemStep style='margin-top:0cm;margin-right:0cm;margin-bottom:0cm; margin-left:2.0cm'>(4)&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;完成后，即可按照该部署手册正常安装。</p>
<p class=MsoNormal>&nbsp;</p>
</div></DIV>
